Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ecology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 101 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 758 and intersection form of rank 3.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the ecology space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 196 and the convergence rate is O(5/n^2).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to ecology theory, we decompose the problem into 196 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
